## 2026-09-13 — Points summary one-at-a-time arrows

- Changed the Sound Points summary area from a multi-card horizontal strip to a one-at-a-time readout with previous/next arrows on either side.
- Restored the readout styling closer to the original turbine labels: a bare glowing text/readout treatment with a soft dark aura instead of the newer boxed card strip.
- Kept Total Points first, then cycles through Intensity, Consistency, Recovery, Knowledge, Volume, and Technique.
- Preserved the cleaner turbine animation by keeping the old turbine-adjacent readouts hidden in the active Points panel.
- Updated the runtime stylesheet import version to `rewards-points-one-at-time-20260913`.
- Validation: CSS brace check, `git diff --check`, and targeted quiet ESLint passed. Browser automation confirmed the mounted Points subtree contains the new one-at-a-time readout and arrow controls; full visual measurement was limited because the in-app browser idled the dropdown closed and its DOM is read-only for force-showing hidden panels.
- Follow-up alignment fix: centered the one-at-a-time readout inside a fixed middle lane and balanced the decorative bolt with an empty opposite track, so the value/label/meta sit on the true center axis between the arrows. Updated the runtime stylesheet import version to `rewards-points-one-at-time-aligned-20260913`.
- Follow-up interaction/space fix: isolated the summary arrow pointer events from the parent reward carousel drag handler so previous/next clicks cycle the point types, then expanded the active Points turbine grid and Tesla scene vertically so the animation uses more of the available panel space. Updated the runtime stylesheet import version to `rewards-points-arrows-turbines-20260913`.
- Follow-up typography fix: stacked the Points panel eyebrow above the Sound Points title and enlarged the one-at-a-time point readout value, label, and meta text while keeping the arrows in their side lanes. Updated the runtime stylesheet import version to `rewards-points-large-text-stack-20260913`.
- Follow-up popup contrast/indicator fix: added a real dark translucent Rewards popup surface behind the busy reward carousel, reset the Points readout to Total Points every time the popup opens, normalized the Gems top indicator scale so it no longer appears squished, and made the active top reward indicator occupy the center slot with the neighboring indicators on either side. Updated the runtime stylesheet import version to `rewards-popup-contrast-reset-indicators-20260913`.
- Follow-up active-popup focus fix: added a subtle header dim layer behind open rewards popups so the active popup reads clearly, and paused both the chyron's text animation and JS headline rotation while a rewards popup is open. Updated the runtime stylesheet import version to `rewards-popup-dim-paused-chyron-20260913`.
- Follow-up aura color fix: matched every one-at-a-time point readout aura to its turbine palette: Total/Tesla blue, Intensity red, Consistency yellow, Recovery green, Knowledge violet, Volume cyan, and Technique orange. Updated the runtime stylesheet import version to `rewards-point-auras-turbine-colors-20260913`.
- Follow-up aura saturation fix: removed the fixed blue from the one-at-a-time readout aura gradient and strengthened the current point color with higher opacity plus colored drop-shadows, so each point type reads as its turbine color instead of washing blue. Updated the runtime stylesheet import version to `rewards-point-auras-saturated-20260913`.
- Follow-up aura softening fix: toned down the saturated one-at-a-time readout aura, reduced its spread/shadow, eased the active text weight/shadow, and added bottom breathing room so the glow and text do not feel heavy or clip at the bottom. Updated the runtime stylesheet import version to `rewards-point-auras-soft-20260913`.
- Follow-up aura clipping fix: converted the one-at-a-time aura from an outside halo to a contained internal glow with more vertical lane height, preventing top/bottom cutoff, and enlarged the lower level/meta line. Updated the runtime stylesheet import version to `rewards-point-auras-contained-20260913`.
- Follow-up aura shape fix: redrew the one-at-a-time aura as a smaller centered clipped ellipse instead of a top/bottom-bounded inset glow, removing the flat cap appearance, and enlarged the lower level/meta line again. Updated the runtime stylesheet import version to `rewards-point-auras-ellipse-20260913`.
- Follow-up active turbine fix: wired the highlighted point readout into the Tesla/turbine scene so Total Points keeps every turbine running, while an individual point type runs only its matching turbine and leaves the other turbines visible but idle. Updated the runtime stylesheet import version to `rewards-active-turbine-only-20260913`.
- Follow-up inactive turbine contrast fix: darkened non-highlighted turbines by reducing their WebGL glow/fill/emissive strength and dimming the DOM fallback shells, while keeping Total Points as the all-turbines-running state. Updated the runtime stylesheet import version to `rewards-inactive-turbines-darker-20260913`.
- Follow-up top indicator visibility fix: gave the Points/Coins/Gems reward menu indicators their own visible luminous base/ring above the reward panels and kept the tiny indicator widgets painting even when inactive, so the top reward-menu selectors cannot disappear while their 3D icons load or pause. Updated the runtime stylesheet import version to `rewards-top-indicators-visible-20260913`.
- Follow-up top indicator 3D-only fix: removed the circular button base/dot and circular loading fallback from the reward menu indicators, added early preloading for the coin/gem 3D modules, and kept the selectors visually as pure 3D icons from the start. Updated the runtime stylesheet import version to `rewards-top-indicators-3d-only-20260913`.
- Follow-up invisible 3D indicator fix: marked the reward menu's pure-3D top indicator anchors as shared-stage header-exempt so they remain drawn by the shared WebGL stage while the header/popup state freezes or dims other header widgets. Updated the runtime stylesheet import version to `rewards-top-indicators-3d-exempt-20260913`.
- Follow-up 3D indicator footprint fix: fixed the reward menu's pure-3D indicator anchors measuring as 0px wide inside their grid logo slots, which left the controls clickable but gave the shared WebGL stage no rectangle to paint. Updated the runtime stylesheet import version to `rewards-top-indicators-3d-footprint-20260913`.
- Follow-up active indicator motion/gem size fix: restored animation pausing for inactive Points/Coins/Gems reward-menu indicators now that their 3D anchors keep a real footprint, and enlarged the pure-3D Gems indicator slot/scale so it reads closer to the other top reward icons. Updated the runtime stylesheet import version to `rewards-indicator-active-motion-gem-size-20260913`.
- Follow-up selected point color fix: made the top Points reward indicator recolor its 3D lightning bolt to match the currently selected point type while the Points panel is open, then reset to the default cyan when the rewards popup closes or leaves Points. Updated the runtime stylesheet import version to `rewards-points-indicator-selected-color-20260913`.
- Follow-up Gems safe readout fix: enlarged the active gem count readout over the safe and slowed the Gems reserves orbit/readout cycle from 6.8s to 10.8s so each gem count lingers longer. Updated the runtime stylesheet import version to `rewards-gem-safe-count-slower-scroll-20260913`.
- Follow-up square hero responsive fix: added Body Command Center container-query hooks and compact square/short-card rules so squarer dashboard viewports switch to compact muscle controls and smaller Support Signals rings before the hero contents overlap.
- Follow-up short-height correction: removed the over-compressed alternate Body Command short mode and instead reflowed low-height cards by moving training controls and Support Signals into the side stack, with a tighter summary row so the avatar keeps vertical room.

- Strengthened the welcome benefit-pill highlight with larger scale, brighter aura/sheen effects, stronger sparkles, and made the starting-step number halo bolder and circular.
- Synced the welcome benefit-pill animation so one card owns the combined movement/aura/sparkle effect at a time, and widened the starting-step text visibility window so the label stays readable over the active photo.

- Reworked the welcome utility-link hover treatment into feathered glow edges, with blue for member login and orange for the in-home trainer assessment link.
- Smoothed the welcome crest entrance with a CSS motion rail and added constant benefit-pill bubble motion that bursts outward during each highlighted state.

- Restricted questionnaire real-photo card backgrounds to the training-location/setup question only.

- Feathered both top welcome utility-link hover effects so the blue account aura and orange in-home trainer aura bloom without a hard pill outline.

- Tightened the Step 12 contact form further with smaller field padding, reduced helper spacing, a compact interest checkbox row, and contact-step-only short-viewport rules to keep the send action visible.
- Added tablet/short-viewport density rules for the questionnaire stage so the stacked header, progress rail, top Back control, question copy, and option cards fit inside the modal shell without clipping.
- Enlarged the hover-only utility-link icon layer and clipped it inside a soft rounded mask so the top assessment/account highlights get a bigger white symbol without creating a hard container edge.
- Tightened questionnaire card sizing at tablet and narrow widths so option tiles shrink more gracefully, and reset each newly opened forward step so no card appears preselected when a section first loads.

- Removed the individual rounded and tinted containers from the Recovery, Strength, and Cardio constellation tracks while preserving their aligned labels and session nodes.
- Turned the Training Constellation lane pills into working all-screen filters with an All Tracks reset, removed the remaining visible inner timeline frame, and rebuilt each lane title with a large containerless icon centered behind full-width text.
- Added a compact color-coded horizontal hero-card indicator strip along the cards' inside top edge; the indicators switch panels without consuming layout height or changing the Welcome Member card dimensions.
- Replaced the hero strip's plain bars with small clickable, color-coded icons for Body Command, Training Constellation, Meters, and Challenges, plus a minimal active underline.

## 2026-08-05

- Fixed the hero-card icon row so every icon directly selects and synchronizes its matching card, moved the row into the clear top-right card space away from the eyebrow, and prevented the collapsed header's invisible overflow layer from intercepting those clicks or expanding the header.

- Kept the hero-card selector in a compact top-right corner opposite each card eyebrow, then color-coded every Training Constellation star by Recovery, Strength, or Cardio and connected only matching stars: exactly three vertical track lines on mobile and one horizontal line per track on desktop.

- Reworked Challenges into a width-first, non-scrolling card: compact challenge rows on narrow screens, three challenge columns when space allows, and a shallow four-metric reward summary strip that keeps the entire experience visible.
- Rebuilt Challenges around a square 3-by-3 color-coded category selector; each icon now swaps in three category-specific challenges, while the reward-track action lives inside the selector and the summary updates with the active category.
- Constrained the Challenges category icon grid to the space above Open Rewards so all nine controls stay evenly sized and never overlap the embedded reward action.
- Made the selected challenges become three equal square cards on large screens while preserving the compact stacked treatment on smaller screens, giving the Challenges workspace a balanced full-width grid.
- Matched the large-screen Support Signals layout to Muscle Levels with a vertical circular-loader rail, vertical snap scrolling, and dedicated up/down controls while retaining the compact mobile presentation.
- Enlarged the shared top-right hero-card selector to comfortable 28px compact and 36px large-screen targets, increased the icon sizes, strengthened hover/focus feedback, and expanded the Challenges header reserve so the controls remain easy to click without covering content.
- Aligned each Training Constellation track directly through its node centers, replaced the desktop full-width strokes with clean per-cell segments that stop before and resume after every icon, and added matching dark node masks on mobile so track colors never draw across the stars.
- Split the former combined Meters panel into three independent hero-row cards for Needle Meter, Achievements, and Favorite Workouts, then added Widgets as a dedicated seventh card after Challenges with direct widget shortcuts and its existing drawer action.

## 2026-10-12 heartbeat: durable template retry and revision protection

## 2026-10-12 heartbeat: planning document validation and recovery

- Account cache, cloud payload, and cross-tab cache loads now reuse the nested planning record validator and require safe nonnegative revisions. Invalid cloud records leave usable local data intact and block automatic upload until a valid cloud document can be loaded. An in-flight sync response cannot clear a newly raised data warning.

Current-day summaries and existing MealLog/Hydration views update at date changes via 30-second checks and focus/visibility events; unfinished form dates remain fixed to their selected date. ## 2026-10-12 — Make profile saves fail safely

- ProfileProvider now persists before changing shared state; Profile identity and automatic saves catch storage/account failures and keep unsaved inputs without marking a successful snapshot. Removed the pre-save shared-state update and obsolete local-only persistence comment.
- Profile reads use the initialized account planning document. Unclaimed legacy browser profile/points no longer become another account's identity/rewards; original legacy bytes remain available for explicit import. Points read only the scoped profile field.
- Profile writes reject callbacks captured for a different account or an uninitialized account; removed the silent avatar-dropping fallback. Profile events refresh the current account store instead of trusting arbitrary event detail.
- Regression checks: 97 planning/database tests pass, including profile quota rollback, no success notification on failure, account-switch rejection, legacy preservation, and isolated points. Production build passed TypeScript and all 132 pages. Targeted lint passed with zero errors; 30 existing Profile page/provider warnings remain (unused helpers, hook/accessibility/image warnings). Browser error log was empty.
- Read-only browser verification: /dashboard/profile loads the existing member identity and shows scoped points. No member fields were edited or saved for testing. Existing dashboard header reward placeholders (1,200 points / tokens etc.) still require a separate real-data pass; this change does not implement a rewards ledger.

## 2026-10-12 — Complete shared website record reads

- Replaced silent 100/500-session and 200-message read caps in lib/member-services.ts with immutable-id cursor pagination (200 requested rows/page; continue until an empty page even under smaller backend caps). Sessions & booking, both calendars, and coach thread now use the same complete bounded reads.
- Each page checks the captured authenticated member, row owner, advancing unique identity, and required display fields. Later failures/invalid rows/account switches reject the full operation; existing callers retain their previous cache with unavailable/retry status. Per-query 12-second and total 60-second deadlines plus a 100,000-row ceiling bound resource use. Reads strip ownership metadata before exposing display models and sort by real timestamps with stable identity ties.
- Balance reads verify profile identity; null, nonnumeric, fractional, negative, and nonfinite credits remain unavailable instead of appearing as usable credits. Zero remains valid.
- Website schema reviewed read-only: supabase/member-sessions.sql and member-messages.sql. No website source changes, production writes, or migration changes. Message validation matches the website's member/coach authors and nonempty 2,000-character bounds. Concurrent website changes are picked up on the next refresh; pagination is not a database transaction snapshot.
- Regression coverage: 101 planning/database tests, including 603 sessions/243 messages under a 37-row backend cap, later-page corruption/failure/account changes, duplicate/nonadvancing cursors, message field validation, and invalid/foreign balance profiles. Targeted member-services lint passed with no warnings/errors; final production build passed TypeScript and generated all 132 pages. The initial build caught the Supabase dynamic-select result type; the parser now explicitly treats page data as unknown before runtime validation.

## 2026-10-12 — Guard stale Goals and Profile editors

Both automatic/profile identity saves compare that baseline; a stale profile cannot undo newer goal projections or other profile changes. Loading reads the current store directly rather than potentially stale provider state.

Undo checks the post-delete revision/account and persists before announcing success, preserving newer saves and failed-write originals. ## 2026-10-12 — Preserve older phase records and stabilize identity

## 2026-10-12 — Confirm cloud workout saves and replay canonical completion

- Workout RPC acknowledgements now require an actual UUID. Null, missing, object/array, numeric, or malformed responses are no longer converted to strings and marked synced. Local completion stays saved and queued for safe retry.
- saveCompletedWorkoutLogWithFallback replays the first persisted PlanningLog and its original session/assignment identity, title, timestamps, and entries. A stale caller retry cannot upload values different from the device completion. Confirmed canonical workouts return the original result without another RPC.
- Account switches during fallback saves return failure with no old-account entries or success id, while the original account's saved queue remains intact. The logger's existing captured-account checks remain in place.

Production build passed TypeScript and all 132 pages; targeted persistence lint is clean. No real member workouts were logged for testing.

## 2026-10-12 — Recover older invalid workout upload confirmations

- Added a shared planningLogUploadConfirmed predicate requiring a valid cloud UUID and parseable confirmation timestamp. The original session id, completion values, and exercise stats remain intact; no load-time migration rewrites member records.

## 2026-10-12 — Guard workout confirmations against in-flight record changes

- Added canonical per-workout JSON revisions. Foreground completion and background replay capture the exact uploaded record version; markPlanningLogSynced checks the current record before writing its confirmation. Changed or removed records cannot receive a late acknowledgement for older data. Nested JSON field ordering does not create false conflicts, and unrelated new completions do not invalidate a workout's revision.
- Foreground confirmation/storage failures return a structured failure with no stale success id or entries, preserving the saved account data for review/retry. Background failures leave the original queue available. This does not attempt to rewrite an already uploaded immutable cloud session after conflicting data changes.
- Verification: 121 planning/database tests pass, covering delayed foreground updates/deletions, changed nested entries during background replay, unrelated completion preservation, and canonical revision ordering. Targeted persistence/log lint is clean. Final-source production build passed TypeScript and all 132 pages. Logger source confirms failed saves retain the draft for review/retry. No real member records were changed for tests.

## 2026-10-12 — Recheck queued workouts before sending stale snapshots

- Background replay now compares each snapshot with its current saved record immediately before upload. Later queued records changed, removed, or confirmed during an earlier request are skipped rather than uploading the old snapshot or creating another unnecessary RPC.
- Canonical foreground/background uploads pass the captured per-workout version through authentication and validate it just before the cloud RPC. Changes during asynchronous authentication cause no workout write. Foreground checks the revision on both successful and failed cloud responses, so a stale attempt cannot return an old local completion as success after the canonical record changes.
- Shared assertPlanningLogVersion supplies consistent confirmation/send guards. Standalone legacy history uploads retain their existing separate behavior because they do not have canonical planning records.
- Verification: 123 planning/database tests pass, including three later-queue mutation cases and both foreground/background changes during authentication; tests assert no stale workout RPCs and exact current-record preservation. Targeted lint is clean. Final-source production build passed TypeScript and all 132 pages. No production/member data was modified.

## 2026-10-12 — Preserve workout numeric meaning across device and cloud

- Replaced permissive parseInt conversion with strict shared numeric mapping. Scientific notation such as 1e2 stays 100 rather than becoming 1. Valid decimal weights and explicit weight zero remain intact; blank/whitespace/null/undefined numeric fields stay unknown (null), not fabricated zero.
- Sets/reps require positive whole numbers within PostgreSQL integer range. Malformed/mixed strings, booleans, hex syntax, non-finite/negative values, fractional counts, and overflow produce a structured error instead of partial parsing or silent null substitution.
- New local completions validate numeric entries before the atomic log/stats write; failed validation creates neither record nor stats and returns failure through the foreground save. Cloud mapping returns structured failure before the RPC. Existing older invalid completions are preserved and stay unconfirmed rather than uploading changed numbers. No automatic repair or deletion is performed.
- Verification: 127 planning/database tests pass, including direct mapping, unknown versus explicit zero, invalid input with no local/cloud writes, original-record preservation, and scientific-notation end-to-end payload equality. Targeted persistence lint is clean. Final-source production build passed TypeScript and all 132 pages. No member data was changed for testing.
- Remaining review: older invalid numeric records need a deliberate review/correction flow; the current queue preserves them and stops on the first failed upload.

## 2026-10-12 — Surface workout upload diagnostics and avoid invalid-record queue blocking

- Revision-guarded markPlanningLogSyncError saves a bounded per-record diagnostic without changing completion values/stats. Identical repeated errors avoid another write; confirmed workouts ignore late errors. Successful confirmation clears the error. Foreground offline completion retains its durable local success if diagnostic storage fails.
- Background replay preflights saved numeric values and empty exercise lists. Invalid records retain their originals with a review reason, while later valid workouts can upload. Cloud/auth/connection failures record a retry reason and stop the queue rather than sending repeated failing requests.
- Workout History displays upload attention reasons for unconfirmed records. The logger now shows the actual save failure instead of replacing it with a generic browser message; failed saves retain the draft.
- Verification: 131 planning/database tests pass, including invalid-first/valid-second replay, no duplicate stats, bounded/idempotent guarded diagnostic writes, quota/stale protection, offline/thrown queue failures, and error clearing after successful retry. Read-only Workout History browser check renders the actual empty account history with no console errors. Targeted lint has no errors and two existing logger effect-state warnings. React review: no new effects/state; diagnostics render as escaped text with status semantics and use the existing planning subscription. Initial build identified a nullable diagnostic message; fallback corrected. Final-source production build passed TypeScript and all 132 pages; 131 tests pass after the fix. No member workouts were created as tests.

## 2026-10-12 — Validate saved goals before plan creation and harden recommendation context

- Added pure goalNumbers.ts with consistent decimal/scientific-notation parsing and numeric target validation. Booleans/arrays/hex/mixed text/nonfinite values cannot masquerade as targets. Optional blank/omitted targets remain optional; current weight now receives the same positive range validation as goal weight.

This closes the presence-only gate for malformed imported goals. Historical goal deadlines alone do not block a new weekly plan; editing/saving Goals still validates its deadline normally.

Only full weekday names or exact three-letter abbreviations are accepted, with whitespace/case normalization and deduplication. Numeric age rejects boolean/array/hex coercion. Pain/soreness/sleep/injury inputs must be reported numeric values within their existing field ranges; blank whitespace no longer becomes an invented zero-hour sleep report. Existing recommendation thresholds remain unchanged.

Targeted lint is clean. Final-source production build passed TypeScript and all 132 pages. No member goals/profile/plans were changed for tests.

## 2026-10-12 — Validate checkout return origin and malformed requests

- Reviewed app checkout against the backend audit. Enrollment is intentionally public before signup and already selects Stripe prices from server plan configuration; no user-id metadata claim was introduced. Website auth/fulfillment reuse remains separate work.
- Removed untrusted Origin-header return URL construction. checkoutOrigin uses APP_URL (then NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL), validates origin-only URLs, refuses credentials/paths/query/fragments/non-HTTP schemes, requires HTTPS/non-loopback configuration in production, and allows only loopback fallback for unconfigured development. Caller Origin, when present, must match that configured origin; cross-site/null/malformed origins return 403 before Stripe is called. Missing/invalid server configuration returns 503.
- Request JSON is validated as an object with a recognized string planId; null, arrays, primitives, malformed JSON, and invalid plan values now return 400 rather than throwing. Online Coaching remains the only enabled enrollment plan.
- No Stripe sessions/payment transactions were created and no production settings were changed. Existing webhook still logs signed events without durable membership fulfillment; app/website paid identity linking and idempotent entitlement fulfillment remain unfinished. Production must configure the intended app origin; preview origins need explicit environment configuration rather than inheriting an arbitrary browser Origin.
- Verification: 156 planning/database/checkout tests pass, including route-level malformed-body and cross-origin refusal before payment creation. Targeted lint is clean. Final-source production build passed TypeScript and all 132 pages.

## 2026-10-12 — Verify enrollment receipt and remove false activation claims

- Confirmation previously accepted payment_status from any retrieved Stripe checkout and selected its displayed membership from the plan query string. checkEnrollmentSession now requires the enabled Online Coaching subscription metadata, the configured server price, exactly one quantity-one line item with complete coverage, a completed session, a confirmed paid/no-payment-required state, and a subscription identity before returning a verified receipt. Open/expired/unpaid sessions remain unpaid; malformed/unrelated/configuration-mismatched records remain unverified.
- Confirmation retrieves expanded line_items and validates bounded checkout session-id syntax before Stripe reads. Verified plan metadata controls paid display and signup handoff; unverified session ids are not forwarded as verified enrollment context.
- Paid receipt copy no longer claims membership entitlements are already active: account setup and durable fulfillment still need to be connected. Unverified states use caution styling and explicitly say verification failed; members who already paid are directed to retain their receipt/contact Sound Fitness before paying again. Success icon/style is reserved for verified payment.
- Reference: Stripe Checkout Session object and retrieve documentation (https://docs.stripe.com/api/checkout/sessions/object and https://docs.stripe.com/api/checkout/sessions/retrieve). These receipt checks do not grant entitlements or replace idempotent webhook fulfillment.
- No real payments were initiated, queried, or changed during verification. Remaining backend work: durable signed-event fulfillment, website/account identity linking, and an authenticated entitlement read used by protected member access.
- Verification: 157 planning/database/checkout tests pass, covering exact price/plan/quantity, complete vs open/expired/unpaid, missing subscription/configuration/line coverage, allowed no-payment-required completion, and invalid session ids. Targeted lint is clean. Final-source production build passed TypeScript and all 132 pages.

## 2026-10-12 — Require exact supported goal directions

- Replaced inherited-object alias lookup and substring matching with a shared exact goal normalizer. Canonical labels and the six known legacy aliases accept case/whitespace normalization; unrelated phrases and inherited names such as toString/constructor/__proto__ never become goal values.

Failed saves preserve both original goals and projected profile; rejected plan creation performs no writes.
- Recommendations normalize goal inputs before dictionary lookup and label missing/unsupported primary goals as a General Health preview. Secondary-only context cannot silently become a chosen primary. Profile/age/recovery recommendation behavior and thresholds remain unchanged.

## 2026-10-12 — Preserve drafts changed during workout completion

- Completed workout records are saved before a cloud-upload await. If a second editor changes the draft during that wait, the stale editor correctly refuses finalization; draft discovery now keeps those additional values visible instead of hiding every draft with a completed session id.
- Saved workout drafts identifies these records as review/export only, explains that History is unchanged, and disables automatic resume. Original template snapshots and exact values remain available for account/revision-guarded JSON export. Successfully finalized drafts with a matching completion timestamp stay hidden.
- Default workout launch now requires a matching actual completion and finalization timestamp before resetting its draft. Missing or mismatched markers preserve the original values; completed default conflicts also appear for review/export. Legacy completed string identities retain their previous reset behavior. Explicit conflict correction/discard and starting a fresh default while preserving a conflict as a separate archive remain future work.

## 2026-10-12 — Refuse logger finalization against an existing completion

- Logger now runs assertWorkoutNotCompleted against local and loaded cloud history before session creation or completion persistence. A matching session id or plan assignment refuses finishing and directs the member to History while preserving current draft values. This prevents an already-completed assignment's immutable persistence replay from being presented as a newly finalized caller session with the wrong hash/id.

- Session-key refs are now assigned only after initial draft persistence succeeds. A quota failure can no longer leave a newly generated key treated as durable on the next retry.

Fallback still preserves the device completion and bounded sync diagnostic, but reports success=false for this conflict so Logger does not clear context/finalize its draft or navigate as though its upload were accepted. Offline/network failures retain ordinary local-success fallback behavior.

Conflicted originals remain available for History review/export; automatic merge/replacement or deletion was not added. Reconciliation UI and canonical cloud-vs-device record comparison remain unfinished.
